29 Oct 2026

Binance hot-wallet drain

Wiki id binance-hot-wallet-drain-oct-2026 · Live incident page

Summary

On 29 Oct 2026 the Solana hot wallet 5tzFkiKscXHK5ZXCGbXZxdw7gTjjD1mBwuoFbhUvuAi9 (“Binance 2” label; attribution only) sent 970,521.9 SOL (~$95.8M at drain-time price, DefiLlama) to attacker AffzFDKZbyMiU11zmVBoAqD25ugpr7xoWWyRyGigBEaU in 5 transfers from 2026-10-29T20:10:18Z to 2026-10-29T20:33:29Z. No SPL tokens were taken. The 5 drain txs are bare legacy System transfers. The wallet’s 1,125 normal withdrawals in the same window used a durable nonce plus compute-budget instructions, so the drain did not come through Binance’s normal withdrawal pipeline. As of 2026-11-01T02:04:53Z the attacker still held 970,554.16 SOL and had signed no transactions. Starting 2026-11-01T09:38:28Z, AffzFDKZbyMiU11zmVBoAqD25ugpr7xoWWyRyGigBEaU signed its first outbound txs. The 7 SOL test and 485,270 SOL went to fresh wallet Eze86Ngj2Ue4xKBbTHgXbhm22xnVF4aTxKrhUuVNDrrD (still unmoved). 5,883.78 SOL then went to hop wallet 7SDuVQ2SkaHBURbxzyXAr8coNcLV2ZuSLkteoRYfQ2fK. After ~2026-11-01T11:12Z AffzFD signed 3 more transfers to 7SDu (2,550.520855619, 1,529.672190908, and 2,488.589267303 SOL). After ~2026-11-01T13:05Z AffzFD signed 4 more transfers to 7SDu (1,323.092486236, 1,225.232561376, 1,225.232561376, and 2,347.398475574 SOL; ~6,120.96 SOL) and 10 SOL to a new hop 4ZpYMF7LqtZ6t6HN7rytWrZBCKBLHJWBudZzPLXyiQ4Y at 2026-11-01T18:56:13Z. Total out of AffzFD on 2026-11-01: 503,860.52 SOL in 13 transfers. 7SDu kept using single-use wallets (500 SOL each, 499.9 deposited) into Relay depository 99vQwtBwYtrqqD9YSXbdum3KBdxPAVxYTaQ3cfnJSrN2. Relay’s Requests API reports 10 more successful requests 2026-11-01T11:23Z–12:37Z (4,999 SOL became 6.06938539 BTC, BTC blocks 974121–974127) and 10 further successful requests 13:05Z–14:33Z (4,799.09 SOL became 5.81469868 BTC, BTC blocks 974130–974137, all from the same Relay solver). Context

This is the Solana side of a multi-chain incident of about $6.3B (Binance’s figure). Per-chain on-chain split: EVM about $3.82B, BTC about $1.47B, Tron about $0.63B, XRP about $0.30B, SOL about $0.10B. Other chains are covered in the living report.

Reported by Binance: a compromised DevOps engineer’s laptop led to a service container that submitted unauthorized withdrawals, with “no evidence of any wallet private key leak”. Later updates spoke of “multiple factors” and “additional points of breach”. Those claims are not independently verified here.

On Ethereum, Binance 14’s own key signed the EIP-7702 authorizations. The chain cannot prove no key leaked.

Attribution: On 2026-10-31 the FBI, together with USSS and IRS-CI, attributed the theft of more than $6B from Binance (on or about 2026-10-29) to the Democratic People’s Republic of Korea (North Korea). The FBI names this activity “TraderTraitor”. The FBI’s published address list includes exactly one Solana address, AffzFDKZbyMiU11zmVBoAqD25ugpr7xoWWyRyGigBEaU, the Solana drain recipient (alongside 608 ETH and 19 BTC addresses). Earlier TRM and Chainalysis claims attributing the incident to DPRK with high confidence are now consistent with the FBI attribution.

On-chain evidence

Every drain is a legacy tx signed solely by 5tzFkiKscXHK5ZXCGbXZxdw7gTjjD1mBwuoFbhUvuAi9, which is also the fee payer. Instruction is one bare system::transfer. Recent blockhash, no durable nonce, no ComputeBudget instructions, 5,000-lamport fee.

In the window 19:30–22:00Z the source had 1,615 txs and 513 SOL-outflow txs. All 1,125 normal outflows use advanceNonce (durable nonce, authority 5tzFki…) plus 2 ComputeBudget instructions plus transfer(s). The 5 drains are the only outflows with a different shape. So they were signed by the hot-wallet key but built outside Binance’s normal withdrawal tx builder.

Lookalike-address dust sent to the hop wallets is third-party address poisoning, not attacker flow. Press (Lookonchain, via crypto.news) said 486,970 SOL was being swapped to BTC via Relay; that is not supported on chain for the ~21:00Z Nov 2 window: only 11,136.247475 SOL went through Relay to BTC from the 7SDu branch, and no on-chain figure equals 486,970. The 485,277 SOL lot at Eze86 later returned to AffzFD (this window) and was fanned out; it did not go through Relay. A reported 1,012,715 SOL cumulative out of AffzFD is not supported on chain: AffzFD out since 2026-11-01T09:38Z is ~679,000 SOL (557,191.96 + 121,804.79). Reported claims that AffzFD “sent tokens” to memecoin dev wallets ($NOTSAFU, $LAZARUS, $TT) are false: those signatures are third-party dust / ATA / memecoin-bait ops, not by the holder. Memecoin airdrops into AffzFD are ignored. The attacker also holds spam tokens. No SPL tokens were taken in the drain.

Root cause (reported)

Reported by Binance: a compromised DevOps engineer’s laptop led to a service container that submitted unauthorized withdrawals, with “no evidence of any wallet private key leak”. Later updates spoke of “multiple factors” and “additional points of breach”. Those claims are not independently verified here.

On-chain, the 5 drains were signed by the hot-wallet key but built outside Binance’s normal durable-nonce withdrawal pipeline. That is consistent with a compromised signing service being handed raw txs. It is equally consistent with direct key use. Chain cannot tell the two apart and does not prove that keys were or were not leaked, so “no key leak” stays Binance’s claim. On Ethereum, Binance 14’s own key signed the EIP-7702 authorizations; that also cannot prove the key did not leak. Binance has not published a root-cause statement specific to its Solana hot wallet 5tzFkiKscXHK5ZXCGbXZxdw7gTjjD1mBwuoFbhUvuAi9.

Lessons

A hot-wallet signer that will sign a bare System transfer is a withdrawal-pipeline bypass. Policy has to sit at the signer (shape, destination, amount), not only in the builder that produces durable-nonce withdrawals. Chain cannot distinguish a compromised signing service from a leaked key.

Coverage gaps

  • How the signing service was induced to sign a bare System transfer is unpublished.
  • Whether the hot-wallet key also leaked cannot be determined on chain.
  • Last normal durable-nonce withdrawal is placed at 2026-10-29T20:36:42Z; the signature is not in this draft.
  • Same-route consolidation of 558,998 SOL at 04:20Z that day is described in the chain notes without a signature in this draft.
  • Other labelled Solana wallets showed no drain activity in the window; unlabelled wallets were not scanned.
  • USD is DefiLlama drain-time price, not independently recomputed here.
  • Non-Solana chains are out of scope of this wiki entry (see the living report), except the Relay BTC fills recorded above and the three ~21:00Z Relay fills (Tron USDT, Ethereum ETH test, Ethereum USDC) whose out-txs were re-checked on those chains.

  • 7SDu’s 8 send signatures other than the hashed Relay deposits are not in this draft. Third-level destinations 4G8iMH (500 SOL), 3ux5vu (600 SOL), and FC1Sc (199.99 SOL) were not walked. Truncated 7SDu hop ids include 3g2Ufm…, 72PtKf…, EfQQxv…, ERaBVwLC…, Mqsh…, 41VFo6…, 3YyQGb…, and 5xbp6K….
  • mSOL at EyaSjUtS… (from 71JD6x’s 4 Marinade deposits into MarBms…) was not walked; those 4 deposit signatures are not in this draft. AmkFno’s 5 sends to 62dhtC and the later 40.1 / 10.1 / 41 / 115.185 SOL sends (GwQvbp…, 6SQWzL…, FwF8Cv…, FkSKWj) lack signatures in this draft. EhWiCX’s 30 SOL multi-DEX swap signature is truncated (3JVueefg…). 6SQWzL’s 5 SOL → 460.08 USDC Jupiter swap to C55EEi3W… is not hashed. GaBzy3 onward hops rg5xRd… and 5H4tcN… are truncated.
  • A reported X claim (@wirisrk) that a 200 SOL 7SDu peel chain ended at 7xRJ… → 6H7G… at 19:38–19:50Z is not verified here.
  • The TJap3i → TVjuTE Tron onward txid is truncated (949ff0332b7c9dcd…). 4BcRUc’s 5.69 SOL balance was not re-checked at ~21:00Z; 4BcRUc received a further 1,406.29 + 890.32 SOL on Nov 3 and its resting balance is not in this snapshot.
  • No Relay (99vQwt…) deposits from the walked wallets in the ~13:05Z Nov 3 window; no Bridgers program ID confirmed; no CEX deposit address identified.
  • A reported X claim (@wirisrk) that Cw1XRJ78LPFLh9P7bpGUSMjkgut1KUCotmjPDotpDaMG swapped its last 2,409 SOL for 222,235 USDC then CCTP (Cw1XRJ now 0.327 SOL, last tx ~04:34Z Nov 3, not walked), and that “~37,179 SOL [was] off Solana at 04:59Z”, is not verified here. The one CCTP path decoded in this window came back to Solana.
  • Level-3+ destinations of the many empty second-level wallets were not exhaustively walked. Walked to level 3 only for the 32 biggest second-level wallets (those third-level balances ~5,935 SOL, spread thin; only UefNb6z6… 11.0 and GyCMG9hQ… 101.5 hold more than 10). Remaining first-hop peel signatures under 5,000 SOL are listed in the ~13:05Z timeline; second-level forwarding of the 28 first-hop wallets is not hashed here except FoRNTj → 7EuBfbi and the 4AV2Qzp deposits.
  • Program 9fhQBbum… is Privacy Cash in this window (75,499 SOL, 117 deposits). The earlier 480.9 SOL into program-owned 4AV2Qzp used the same program; the X Bridgers label on that earlier window remains unverified. A deposit-to-withdrawal link through the pool is not claimable. Do not conflate with the earlier truncated FDF8AxHB… deposits (~244.3 SOL).
  • Marinade mSOL laddering (~278 MarBmsSg… interactions since 18:32Z Nov 2) is churn; individual deposit/withdraw signatures and the EyaSjUtS… destination were not walked.
  • Burn-and-reissue applies only to issuer tokens. Native SOL cannot be frozen or burned by an issuer. USDC could be frozen by Circle only while it sits on Solana or EVM; none was frozen here, including the FoRNTj CCTP USDC that returned to Solana.
  • Binance has not published a root-cause statement specific to its Solana hot wallet 5tzFkiKscXHK5ZXCGbXZxdw7gTjjD1mBwuoFbhUvuAi9 (4.0456 SOL as of ~13:05Z).
